For it is a powerful <term>mass</term> and a high <term>gradation</term> <term>gradation: the process of refining a substance to its highest state of power</term>, beyond which nothing can be higher; for by its speed and strength, Christ signified the advent of his [future] coming. <note>A reference to Matthew 24:27: "For as the lightning cometh out of the east, and shineth even unto the west; so shall also the coming of the Son of man be."</note>

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Furthermore, when such an indication appears, it may well be that it dissipates and nothing comes of it except rain, which is born of the wind itself and carried to another place. For it happens <margin>NB</margin> <note>NB: Latin "nota bene," meaning "note well"</note> that the <term>mass</term>, before it is fully prepared, shifts to and fro, just like ground gunpowder that, when ignited, shudders here and there. It does not go "upward," whatever is in the <term>Firmament</term> <term>Firmament: The sky, envisioned here as a physical vault that reflects or mirrors the Earth</term>. For everything that goes upward on Earth, goes "downward" in the Firmament. And this going downward is the Firmament's "upward," just as the Earth's "upward" is the Firmament's "downward." <note>The author is describing a mirrored or inverse relationship between the terrestrial and celestial realms</note> Therefore, it inclines more and more toward the Earth, and more and more with a smoke, as is the nature of this matter.

# The Book /

But when the <term>perfection</term> <term>perfection: the final stage of a process where a substance reaches its full potential or completed state</term> begins—that is, when the <term>mass</term> <term>mass: the concentrated "materia" or substance in the clouds composed of sulfur, saltpeter, and mercury</term> in that place enters into a single beam, in such a way that it unites and perfects itself into its own mass—then know that this same mass is nothing else but a well-sealed casing that holds itself together. The closer it comes to its "birth," the firmer its skin becomes, and at last, when it is fully ripe, it lies within its shell just like an egg in its shell.

Now, when it is prepared in this way and lies within such a shell, it follows that the other <term>vaporous masses</term> <term>vaporous masses: "Gewülcke," referring to the surrounding clouds or billows of moist air</term> that are nearby are born only for rain; these dissolve themselves into water. And as soon as this <term>resolution</term> <term>resolution: the process of a solid or vaporous substance turning into liquid</term> begins, the wind and <term>impetus</term> <note>original: "impetus," meaning violent force or sudden momentum</note> also move through the mass in every way. Its vaporous masses are all born for the <term>tempest</term> <note>original: "tempestat," meaning a violent storm</note>, to be brought to completion with turbulence. 

When the mass lying in the shell feels the moisture from the vaporous masses—that is, when the moisture reaches the mass—it is exactly as if one were to pour water into molten <term>bell-metal</term> <term>bell-metal: "glocken speise," a brittle alloy of copper and tin; pouring water onto it while molten causes a violent, explosive reaction</term> or something similar. As soon as the water touches the mass, it breaks apart into all parts of the world, and in that same crack, it produces a flash of lightning <note>original: "himlizen," a quick, nimble flash on the horizon</note>, swift and agile. From that moment on, as many pieces as break, so many <term>thunders</term> occur; every crack is a thunderclap. # OF METEOROLOGY

In the same way, because the **Devil** possesses humans and makes them so violent—as is publicly known—and has the power to possess livestock, such as swine <note>A reference to the Gerasene demoniac in the Gospels, where Jesus permitted demons to enter a herd of pigs</note>: if this is permitted to him, then he is also permitted [to possess] dogs, cats, horses, and everything a human owns. And he is also permitted to possess the **storms and lightning**, <note>original: "gewetter vñ Stral"</note> showers, and hail, and to create such **turbulence**, <note>original: "vngestümigkeit"</note> and diligently to shoot them wherever he pleases, as God permits.

Regardless of how it all may be, it happens by the **permission of God**. In this, it is well to remember that He will also send the Devil onto the earth so that he may take his own into the **eternal fire** with a great violence. In the wisdom and will of God, nothing is to be attributed to a human, but rather to be reflected upon; for this reason I am prompted that He has modeled Himself therein, and will not **break a bruised reed** <note>A reference to Isaiah 42:3 and Matthew 12:20, signifying God’s mercy toward the fragile and repentant</note> nor **quench a smoldering wick**: <note>original: "glüenden putzen"; refers to the glowing snuff of a candle wick about to go out</note> but indeed the other, which is whole and burning high. This is then well understood in the thundering and storms.

Thus the origin of the **lightning bolt** and the **storm clouds** <note>original: "Gewülcke"</note> (likewise) in the firmament toward the earth is a **wondrous growth**, <note>original: "gewachs"; meaning a natural phenomenon or formation</note> and even more wondrous in its workings.

## CONCERNING STRANGE THINGS
### Chapter IX.

<margin><insert>VB</insert></margin>
Furthermore, it is also necessary to know about the **generation** <note>original: "geberung"; refers to the biological production or birthing of creatures</note> of things such as **frogs, worms**, or other living creatures that are generated and found [falling] from above. Regarding this, know that I mentioned at the beginning concerning animals in heaven that there is no **element** that does not have a living creature within it—that is, many kinds. Just as there are many kinds of worms in the earth, many in the water, and likewise in the air, so too in the **firmament** <note>In the cosmology of the time, the firmament was the crystalline sphere of the heavens containing the stars and planets</note>. Therefore, no one should wonder at such things; **nature is a mother** in the elements, among both the upper and the lower realms.

<margin>Frogs.</margin>
From this it follows that when it is said to have **rained frogs**, such frogs are from the firmament, and in the firmament their like are born from such frogs. For certain reasons, they have come down to the earth. As the example shows, **moles** <note>original: "Schermeuße"; literally "shearing mice," a common historical term for moles or shrews</note> also often come and run upon the earth, though that is not their usual nature, just as is the nature of some worms like-

...and such is also the case. Therefore, it is no less true that these are also found in the heavens, coming forth from their own region.

<margin>Tree Frogs</margin>
<note>original: "Laubefrosche"</note>
Although it is not possible for something to fall down upon us from above—for that is "upward" from us, just as if we were to fall up into the heavens—it is nevertheless true that they do fall down. However, for them, it is "upward," according to the nature of the heavens. Thus, it cannot happen unless a storm causes such **destruction** in those places, and they fall down in that same **violent force** <note>original: "impetu"</note>, so that they enter into the rain and similar conditions. It is also the case that **seed** <note>original: "Sperma"; here referring to biological precursors or "spawn" rather than modern cellular sperm</note> falls down during the month of May, from which **tree frogs** <note>original: "laubfrösche"</note> develop. For all tree frogs originate from the **firmament** <note>the sphere of the stars and upper atmosphere</note> and fall down in the manner of seed.

Therefore, an animal is often found in a place where its parents have never been. In some cases, this is the responsibility of the firmament; in others, the air is the cause. The air contains such **generating principles** <note>original: "generantia"; the active forces or seeds that produce life</note> in many forms within itself, just as much as the earth or the water does. Such is the birth of **basilisks** <note>a legendary reptile said to be hatched from a cock's egg; in this context, any creature born through spontaneous generation</note> and many new, unheard-of animals whose origins are without earthly mothers, contrary to all other animals of the earth and water.

<meta>This page continues the discussion on phenomena descending from the firmament. While the previous page focused on frogs and "seeds" falling from the air, this page explains the chemical-atmospheric process behind "blood rain" and other colored rains, attributing them to the decoction of sulfurous foams in the sky.</meta>

->## THE BOOK<-

It is also no less true—and it often happens—that people say it is raining blood. This occurs in the following way: The **aurora** <note>original: "Morgenrötte"; in this context, it refers to a reddish atmospheric vapor or "foam" rather than simply the dawn</note>, which is a foam of **sulfur** <note>original: "Sulphur"; one of the three primary alchemical principles, associated here with light, heat, and color</note> boiling among the three first principles, often happens to be **decocted** <note>boiled down or concentrated through heat</note> into a **viscous state** <note>original: "Tenacitet"</note> and emerges from the nature of foaming. As has been said of the aurora, it is a light foam. Now, when this same foam takes on the nature of **mucilage** <note>original: "Mucilaginis"; a thick, gluey substance</note> or **bitumen** <note>original: "Bituminis"; a sticky, mineral-tar substance</note>, the first and second result is that it becomes red. For its color is red, and its substance is similar in its thickness or thinness to blood—perhaps a little more or less. Once it is thus **prepared** and **transmuted** <note>chemically transformed from one state to another</note>, the heaven lets it fall. For the heaven carries no **tangible body** <note>original: "greifflich Corpus"</note>; that is, for an intangible thing to become tangible, everything must be separated from it. Thus, it distills downward—sometimes by itself, sometimes with rain, however it may occur. The frogs in the **firmament** <note>the celestial sphere or upper atmosphere</note> are not a substance initially, but they *become* substance, and then they also fall down.

<margin>Colors falling.</margin>
Likewise, it happens that many of the foams and similar **exhalations** <note>vapors or gaseous emissions</note> are incorporated into colors just as they appear, and thus those same colors fall down: white, blue, or black. For the water that falls from the heaven is not of a single color, but rather the colors are distinct from one another.

### OF METEORS

<margin>Raining milk. Usnea.</margin>
It also happens in certain places that it rains milk, in the same manner as the rain of blood. Whatever reaches the stars creates a white, bituminous <term>usnea</term> <note>original: "usneam"; in Paracelsian thought, this refers to a celestial moss or lichen-like residue that forms from atmospheric vapors</note>, which in time coagulates and hardens like a stone that has grown out of another. And such <term>celestial usnea</term> <note>original: "usneæ cœlestes"</note> are of many kinds and in all colors: red—which is the blood rain—green, blue, black, yellow, etc. That which falls upon the earth also disappears into the water; it is also diluted in color by the rain, so that these substances may not reach the earth in the same state as they exist in themselves.

It is no less true that the ancient <term>naturalists</term> <note>original: "Naturales"; scholars who studied the laws of nature</note> understood much regarding such <term>usnea</term> in <term>medicine</term> and also in <term>alchemy</term>, wherever they were able to obtain it. And they called them <term>celestial flowers</term> <note>original: "Flores Cœlestes"</note>. But what was accomplished with them is hidden from me, namely something that their <term>spanditres</term> <note>likely a specialized Paracelsian term referring to specific spiritual powers or emanations of the stars</note> had fathomed. I mean the true <term>naturalists</term>, not the others.

<margin>Raining fire.</margin>
Furthermore, it is also sometimes seen to rain fire, which has the following cause: All rain that exists is nothing other than <term>sulfur</term>, <term>salt</term>, and <term>mercury</term> <note>the "Tria Prima," or three primary principles of Paracelsian alchemy, believed to be the building blocks of all matter</note>. The <term>sulfur</term> <note>original: "Sul." / "Sulphur"</note> is of a burning nature in itself, and must resolve itself from burning into a water. Now, when it happens that it enters the air from its stars and is not yet [completely]...

The **Heavenly Winged Beings** <note>original: "Superi Pennates"; likely referring to elemental spirits or angelic agencies that govern the middle air and atmosphere</note> are the **Carriers** <note>original: "Portores"; those who transport or deliver</note> in such matters. Just as in my time, crosses, roosters, scourges, Pilate, etc., fell upon the people in such a form as has been recorded in writing. <note>The author refers to a famous series of "prodigies" reported in the early 1500s, such as the "showering of crosses" where people found cross-shaped marks appearing on their clothes, interpreted as divine warnings of the Reformation or the plague.</note> However, this happened not out of the ordinary course of Nature, but out of God’s order as a figure of a new **alteration** <note>original: "alteration"; a significant change or transformation in the state of the world</note> within those very things, which such signs precede and accompany, as has then been proven, and still is.

Likewise, such **figures** <note>original: "figuras"</note> also often occur in the heavens, which signify nothing other than the future to which they pertain. For example, in recent years, a "King" was seen in **Silesia** <note>a historical region now mostly in Poland; the author likely refers to a "fata morgana" or complex mirage in the sky interpreted as a royal procession</note> with many strange types and persons. This is a **Presage** that such a thing shall also be seen and fulfilled in its own time. For the **Heavenly Winged Beings** are the masters, workers, preparers, arrangers, and fashioners of such **Presages**.

Therefore, everyone should know that such things are nothing other than out of Nature, through...

<meta>Continuity: Following the previous discussion of the "Heavenly Winged Beings" (Superi Pennates) as messengers who cast signs down to Earth, the author now explains that these omens are divine warnings regarding human morality and describes the elemental "offices" that govern the creation of rain, snow, and even falling stones or metals.</meta>

### METEOROLOGY. 59

The **Heavenly Winged Beings** <note>original: "Pennates superi"</note> were made and commanded from the inner heaven to proclaim the coming of the time. He who would describe the nature and appearance of such strange figures and **Likenesses** <note>original: "Similitudines"; images or omens appearing in the sky</note> could not write enough books, for they are wondrous in their details. Therefore, man should pay more heed to these than to all of nature, for the reason that it touches upon his shame and vice. <note>The author suggests that celestial omens are specifically sent to rebuke human sin.</note> Thus, **Thunders** <note>original: "Tonitrua"</note> are also not without such a wondrous cause; for God is unfathomable in His works, and there is no one who may fathom them to the very end.

<margin>Stones and Metals.</margin>
Now it is no less true that it is also found that stones fall from the sky, and sometimes **Metals**. Note this: God has given to each **Element** its **Prime Matter** <note>original: "Primam materiam"; the foundational, chaotic substance from which specific things are created</note> from which shall be produced (**generated**) that which belongs to it. As the Earth has **Plants** <note>original: "Plantæ"</note>, the Water the **Minerals**, the Air the **Tereniabin** <term>Tereniabin: A Paracelsian term for "heavenly manna" or a sugary dew that was believed to fall from the atmosphere and crystallize</term>, so also the Heaven has its own **generations**. Now each must remain in its own domain and generate nothing else. Therefore, it is not possible that the Water or the Earth generate the lightning bolt, **Rain**, or Snow; the Heaven alone must do it, for that is its **office** <note>original: "officium"; its divinely appointed function or duty</note>. Furthermore, the Heaven may also not generate that which belongs to the others...

<summary>The author explains that celestial phenomena and omens are divine messages concerning human morality and details how each element (Earth, Water, Air, Heaven) has a unique "prime matter" responsible for generating its own specific products, such as plants, minerals, or weather.</summary>

But know now that it has been proven that such a thing has happened: true, natural stones have fallen from the Heavens. It is also to be believed that metals have fallen in the same way, yet none other than Iron. For there is a cause in both [stones and iron] why it cannot be that other things fall down in this manner, except Iron and a certain genus of stone. For it happens thus: 

You see that all **Stars** have an attractive power within them; the **Sun** draws to itself what is moist, while the **Moon** draws to itself what is warm in nature. Thus, the Sun draws the cold to itself—that is, the **Water**, which is visible. Conversely, the Moon draws what is warm—that is, wherever a warm nature exists in a creature, the Moon draws it out. 

<margin>9B</margin>
From this it follows that people from whom the Moon draws warmth—if they are burdened with illnesses—will feel it if the illness is caused by cold, and there is no illness (or only a small one) while the warmth remains. But as soon as the Moon enters its **magnetic mode** <term>Magnetische arth: A property of attraction similar to a magnet; the author believes celestial bodies "pull" elemental qualities out of living things like a magnet pulls iron.</term>, it withdraws the warmth from the person; therefore, he becomes sicker, which would not happen otherwise. ...tle. And what it does not do, because it is not suited to its nature, other stars do in other ways.

Therefore, know now that there are also other stars which possess a **magnetic nature** <term>Magnetische arth: A property of attraction similar to a magnet; the author believes celestial bodies "pull" elemental qualities out of living things or the earth.</term>; these draw **iron** <note>original: "Eyfen" (Eisen)</note> to themselves. Otherwise, no metal can be drawn toward them. Thus, when an **attraction** <term>attraction: The physical act of drawing or pulling an object toward a center</term> begins, it is certain that iron will be drawn upward into the **mansions** <term>Mansiones: The "houses" or fixed stations of the stars in the heavens</term> of that same star. 

Just as the mountain of **magnetite** <term>Magnes: A lodestone or naturally magnetic mineral</term> naturally proves on Earth that it draws iron from its surroundings to itself—though this does not happen all at once in a single large piece, but rather gathers it together in small portions <note>original: "mit der portz"</note>, as far as the iron is able to follow, until it has brought much together—so too does the **magnetic star** <term>Magnes: Used here to describe a star with the property of a magnet</term> draw iron to itself in small forms, from its nearest edges and locations into its **mansion**. 

Once the star possesses the iron, it then **erodes** <term>Radiert: From the Latin "radere," meaning to scrape or shave; here referring to the breaking down of the metal's surface</term> it and melts it down just as a **magnet** does, letting the **rust** <term>Eruginem: Latin "aerugo," referring to metal dross or oxidation</term> fall away from it, and yields a refined **essence** <term>Alcool: In 16th-century alchemy, this refers not to liquid spirits but to the "alcohol of wine" or the finest, most microscopic powder or essence of a substance.</term>. 

And whatever **essence** <term>Alcool</term> exists within the stars (the suns) originates from such **bodies** <term>Corporibus: Physical substances or matter</term> that have been drawn up and **refined** <term>corradiert: Thoroughly scraped or broken down into a pure state</term> into an **essence** by the upper **stars** <term>stellis: Latin for stars</term>.

It is also no less important to know and remember that material from wood is also drawn upward, as well as many other such things. For it is found that when the **essences of salt are collected** <term>Alcool Salis colligiert: The gathering of the finest, most purified salt particles</term>...

But know that **Death** <note>original: "Todt"</note> exists in such **consumption** <term>consumation: The process of being used up or destroyed, often by fire or natural decay.</term>, and it consumes a thing just like fire. 

For this reason, know that it cannot come back upon us nor fall down again, any more than the water that rises because of the sun returns to us; rather, it dies in the air and is entirely consumed.

Therefore, know regarding this: that through **philosophy** <note>original: "Philophey"; refers here to natural philosophy or the science of the era.</note> and the power of the stars, I can rightly consider such things and more. For whatever lies within the sun is more intensely consumed than those things which are not of a **fixed body** <term>fixen Corpus: In alchemy, a "fixed" body is one that resists the action of fire or does not easily evaporate.</term>; that which is not fixed passes away. As an example: no one can deny or say that what fire consumes and takes away, the **Heavens** <note>original: "Himel"</note> might not also do, and in its own time. However, fire takes away in a single hour what **nature** <note>original: "natur"</note> cannot do for itself in many years.

But concerning the iron that falls back down, take note of this manner of thing. The same **Etheric Magnet** <term>Etheric Magnet: A celestial force or "magnetic star" believed to draw earthly elements upward into the sky.</term> draws the iron to itself, as has been said; for since the iron is not **fixed** <note>original: "fix"; in alchemy, a 'fixed' substance resists fire and does not evaporate.</note>, it is transmuted into **rust** <note>original: "Eruginem"; likely from the Latin *aerugo*, referring to the corroded surface or essence of the metal.</note>, which the magnet draws up after the magnet has prepared it for this. Now, when such iron reaches the magnet—if there is too much of it, and **Vulcan** <term>Vulcanus: The alchemical personification of fire and the "internal smith" who transforms matter through heat.</term> is too swift, or if a fire-force enters into it that grips the iron with the **Etheric Flux** <note>original: "Etheriſchen Borras"; likely referring to a celestial version of borax, which was used in smelting to help metals melt and flow.</note>—he can cause it to flow together into one **mass** <note>original: "Maß"</note>, as if it were in a fire-oven or smelting furnace. And once it has thus become a **solid body** <note>original: "Corpus"</note>, the **firmament** <term>Firmament: The physical shell of the heavens, which was thought to be able to support vapors but not heavy, refined solids.</term> can no longer carry it, and lets it fall. Wherever such iron lies or is found, it must be better than other iron, for the Etheric Flux has **purified and refined** <note>original: "geleutert vnd gradiert"</note> it. However, this is no common occurrence; rather, it is nature revealing itself in this way.

It may also well happen that such a generation of iron, having been drawn up from below, is often seized by the **celestial spirits** <term>Pennates: Traditionally Roman household gods; here used to describe invisible elemental or atmospheric forces that "work" the materials in the sky.</term>, who then fabricate things with it and let them fall. For the **beings above** <note>original: "Superi"</note> are within the **magnetic stars**, and their skill is very powerful in such work.

# The Book / ON EXHALATIONS. Chapter 10.

<margin>Shooting stars.</margin>
Now observe further, that the stars cleanse themselves—both summer and winter stars—not with lightning <term>himlitze: An archaic German term for heat lightning or the flashing of the sky without thunder.</term>, but with a **body**; that is, they cast a body out from themselves, which is called a **shooting star** <note>original: "Sterngeschoß," literally "star-shot."</note>. Now the origin is this: they have their nourishment in the **firmament**, so that they sustain themselves from that same **element**. For nothing is created that must not have its daily feeding, just as much as man; from which it follows that they also eat from their own element and derive nourishment. And whatever sustains itself with food and drink must also give off its **waste** <term>Excrementum: Latin for waste matter or dross; here used to describe the byproduct of celestial metabolism.</term>. Now, it follows from this that they are sustained by nothing but **fire**, just as man is sustained by the earth from which he comes. From this same fire they draw out their **essence** <note>original: "Essentiam"</note>, just as man draws his blood and flesh. Whatever is not of the essence, that same thing will be cast off as waste. If this happens by day, no one sees it; if it happens by night, one sees it. If it is a **fiery** star that stretches itself out long, it comes down from above, from the heaven of the stars, but one cannot see how high it

As soon as it touches the earth, however, it is earthly and no longer fire, but a jelly and a slime, like red-colored or yellow-colored **frogspawn** <term>frosleich: Frog eggs; used here to describe the viscous, organic appearance of the residue found on the ground after a meteor sighting.</term>.

In this way, the <term>Pennates</term> <note>original: "Pennates"; celestial spirits or guardian powers believed to inhabit the higher reaches of the atmosphere</note> have enough material in the **Firmament** <note>the heavens or the sky conceived as a solid arch</note> that they may make such **brassy Suns**, as many as they wish, from such brass and other similar things. # METEOROLOGY

...of a strange kind in the Heavens, for all of it is their work.

<margin>Two types.</margin>
Likewise, the <term>Pennates</term> <note>celestial spirits believed to inhabit the atmosphere and create signs</note> also make rings or rainbows around the Sun and Moon, or many other such signs of various colors. They also make rainbows in cases where a rainbow is intended to be an <term>omen</term> <note>original: "præfagium"; a prophetic sign or forewarning</note>. Otherwise, there are other rainbows which are natural. However, because God specifically signified and established a **peace** <note>referring to the biblical Covenant of the Rainbow after the Great Flood (Genesis 9)</note> through the rainbow—where such peace is still to exist or come to pass, or where a peace is to arise—then such a rainbow has [already] passed away. These are made and positioned solely by the Pennates. Therefore, one should distinguish these particular rainbows from the natural ones.

The natural ones occur in this way: they are nothing but <term>Sulfur, Salt, and Mercury</term> <note>original: "Sul. Sal, vnd Merc."; the three fundamental principles of alchemy representing the combustible, the solid, and the fluid/volatile</note>, together with their colors, digested in the best and highest manner at that place. They are born in the **Firmament** from the water-stars when they wish to produce rainy weather; thus, the rainbow is the <term>blossom</term> <note>original: "plust" or "plüest"; a flowering or preliminary state before the fruit (rain) appears</note> of it. And no rain ever comes without a pre-blossom. When a star intends to turn into rain, the rainbow is first its blossom, and the rain follows after.

Now, it is no less the case that they do not appear as often as it rains; that is, they do not all become visible, but rather they vanish into the air and consume themselves.

### The Book /

Where, however, mighty outbursts of rain occur, there mighty rainbows are formed; these sink down from the stars and clouds toward the earth, and stand with their two points upon the ground, while the **curvature** <note>original: "Rotundiert"; refers to the rounded arc or circularity of the rainbow</note> arches upwards, and they remain so long until the air is consumed. And so it often happens that two are formed; these are <term>Gemini</term> <note>Latin for "twins"; here referring to a double rainbow</note>, signifying a rain twice as long as what the single one signifies by itself.

These are God’s order, so provided and ordained by Divine <term>Providence</term> <note>original: "prouidentz"; the foreseeing care and guardianship of God over the world</note>. For there is nothing that does not produce a **blossom** <note>original: "plüest" or "blüest"; a specific term in this philosophy suggesting that every natural event has a preliminary "flowering" stage before it fully manifests</note>: **Heat lightning** <note>original: "Himlizē"; likely referring to *Wetterleuchten*, or flashes of light seen on the horizon without audible thunder</note> is a blossom of the storm and the thunderbolt. Likewise, there are many more kinds of fruits, and many blossoms exist that we may not see and have no knowledge of, because they consume themselves and pass away in the air. We see only that which indeed falls and comes down [to earth], etc.

<summary>This page explains the formation of double rainbows as omens of prolonged rain and introduces the theory that all weather phenomena, including heat lightning, are "blossoms" that precede a final physical result.</summary>

# THEOPHRASTUS PARACELSUS
## Book of the Womb <note>original: "Liber de Matrice"</note>

Now, beyond all that I have said concerning the ailments of humanity, the whole story has not yet been told. For when we reach the foundations of **Medicine**, we find other **Theoretical**, **Physical**, and **Astronomical** matters within a human being, beyond everything that has been presented so far. Now, these same matters concern the **Matrix** <term>Matrix</term> <note>Paracelsus uses this term to refer to the womb, viewing it as a microcosm or "womb of the world" that mirrors the creative powers of nature.</note> alone, relating only to women. 

Since the foundation further on in this book is nothing other than to describe that same Matrix and its ailments—in the same way that a person's ailments would be described—know then that I speak of invisible things. For who is there who has seen in the **Anatomy of the Matrix** <note>original: "Anatomia Matricis"</note> that which I shall present in what follows? 

Although it is true that nothing is visible at that place, it should nevertheless be judged as visible. In the **Air** we see nothing, yet we perceive it; it is the same in the **Mother** <note>original: "Mutter"; here used as a synonym for the womb, the "mother" of all physical development within the body.</note>. Therefore, in the air, we may well examine what we perceive even though we do not see it; for the task of "seeing" is not commanded to the eyes alone, but...

# OF THE WOMB.
### I iij
<meta>catchword: others</meta>

The Microcosm <term>Microcosm</term> <note>original: "kleine Welt"; literally "small world," referring to the human being as a reflection of the entire universe.</note> is, however, the human being; this same being is also enclosed within a skin, so that his blood, his flesh, and whatever else the human being is, should not exist in common with the Macrocosm <term>Macrocosm</term> <note>original: "grossen Welt"; the "great world" or the universe at large.</note>. That is to say, his elements should not touch the external elements in their substance, lest one should destroy the other. For this reason, the human being has the skin upon him; it is the human boundary that separates the two worlds from each other—the great and the small, which is to say, the World and the Human—so that two opposing things do not collapse together into a single world. Thus, the world remains within its own housing, whole and unbroken, and no one is inside that same house to disturb or break it. Likewise, the human being remains in his house, which is to say, in his skin.

Now it follows from this that the human is not the only world, nor is the World alone; rather, there is yet another world, and it is the smallest of all: the Womb <term>Matrix</term> <note>original: "Matrix"; Paracelsus treats the womb not just as an organ, but as a self-contained, third "world" with its own unique laws and structural integrity.</note>. This too is a world unto itself, bound within a vessel; that is, it has its own particular container, skin, and structure, so that it may stand for itself. And it is separated from the Microcosm <note>the "small world" of the human body</note>. Thus, the man is the Microcosm; the woman has within that same world a specific condition <term>gepresten</term> <note>original: "gepresten"; while usually meaning an infirmity, ailment, or "lack," Paracelsus uses it here to define the anatomical and spiritual "distinction" or "addition" that separates the female body from the male Microcosm.</note>. She is the smallest world, and is something other than the man, and has its own...

<summary>Paracelsus explains that skin acts as a barrier between the human "small world" and the universal "great world," then identifies the womb as a "third world"—the smallest of all—which defines the woman's unique nature.</summary>

<header>The Book/</header>

flesh is <term>Salt</term>, <term>Mercury</term>, and <term>Sulfur</term> <note>These are the *Tria Prima*, the three prime substances Paracelsus believed composed all material things.</note>; the earth is also mercury, salt, and sulfur; the sea is also mercury, salt, and sulfur, and so on with the others. She also differs from the man by as much as she is [taken] out of him—that is, by however much she is less in weight. She is from the man, yet she is different; therefore, her <term>Anatomy</term>, <term>Philosophy</term>, <term>Physics</term>, and <term>Theory</term> are also different, yet consistent with the other two; for she is the smallest [world].

# OF THE WOMB.

and the final world. And as far as the <term>Matrix</term> <note>original: "Matrix"; the womb, which Paracelsus treats not just as an organ, but as a generative "world" or "monarchy" within the female body.</note> teaches you to distinguish the woman from the man and how to regard her, so far should you also separate her diseases from the diseases of the man. And consider that what is impossible for the [Macrocosmic] world is possible for the man, and what is impossible for the man is possible for the woman. Therefore, because they are distinct in themselves, so all her illnesses are also distinct in that same way.

Since our eyes grant us insight into the function of these bodies, we must adopt a different <term>Philosophy of Medicine</term> regarding women; her diseases are not to be compared to the diseases of men, nor should we rely on the similarity of signs. For although death is the same in men and women, the man is one thing and the woman another. Even if a physician recognizes a specific disease in both men and women—though death and misery are equal, and thirst and hunger exist in both—the thirst of the man is different from the thirst of the woman, and the hunger is different as well.

For the last is different from the first or the middle; as far as a mother is separated from her son, so far do these things stand apart from each other. # THE BOOK

[...learned] writers, who however mislead others, treat women and men as equal in their illnesses, such as a *paralysis* <note>original: "Paralysis"; a loss of motor function or stroke.</note>, an *apoplexy* <note>original: "Apoplexia"; a sudden loss of consciousness or internal "strike."</note>, an *epilepsy* <note>original: "Caducus"; literally "the falling sickness."</note>, etc. But that is false: the root <note>original: "wurtzen"; the foundational source or cause of a condition.</note> of the woman is one thing, and that of the man is another; therefore, medicine must also be distinct in its understanding of the causes and in the knowledge of the cure. Although in the end it breaks forth—such as the menstruation <note>original: "Menstrum"</note> and the like—these are illnesses that are specifically separated from those of the man. This same distinction is even possible for a peasant to make, so that he knows what a woman is and what he is not. But the physician does not want it so; rather, he is a misleader of medicine and a misleader of the sick, because the physician does not say: "The man's *apoplexy* <note>original: "Apoplexia"</note> comes from this source, from the *subject* <note>original: "Subiecto"; the physical substance or underlying constitution.</note> of such and such, created and given only to the man in this way; [whereas] the woman's *apoplexy* comes out of its root in the <term>womb</term> <note>original: "Matrix"</note>, which lies in such and such a way in her *subject* <note>original: "Subiecto"</note>." If he does not say this, then the physician is no physician at all.

For there are two types of medicine on Earth: one for women and one for men. That of the woman is one thing, and that of the man is another. For the woman, her medicines are good; for the man, his own are good. But he who does not hold this distinction considers the medical art to be like water, the same for everyone.

# The Book

Anatomy <note>In this context, "Anatomy" refers not just to the dissection of a body, but to the fundamental structure and essential nature of all living things as designed by God or Nature.</note> has split all growing things into female and male—and not without reason. It serves as a mirror for the doctor, so that here, in the **Light of Nature** <term>Liecht der natur</term> <note>A central Paracelsian concept referring to the innate wisdom and intuitive understanding of the natural world, granted by God, which allows a physician to see the hidden causes of disease.</note>, he may see how he ought to act. But regardless of all this, the doctor has left out the Light of Nature; he has placed his faith in his own fantasy and has used medicine <term>Artzney</term> in such a way—as if one wanted to see through a wall to what is inside. Just as that is impossible, so too is his approach.

Regarding this assignment, it has its own book [explaining] how all arts were discovered; and how they were found not through such speculation, but through visible experience before one's own eyes. This is not experience born of fantasy, but of the Light: He who sees a thing has experienced it; he who does not see it has not experienced it. 

Thus, we testify to nothing other than what we see, especially in medicine. There, we see two "anatomies" in the **Great World** <note>The Macrocosm or the universe.</note> within all natural growths. Likewise, there are also two in the human being: the woman and the man. Since we see this, we must base our foundation upon it: that there is one **Monarchy** <term>Monarchey</term> <note>Paracelsus uses "Monarchy" to describe a self-contained system of natural laws or a specific kingdom of medical knowledge that governs a particular body or disease type.</note> of the woman, and another of the man, and there is not one single way for both. Furthermore, all the specific conditions <note>original: "zufell"; referring to "accidents" or symptoms that occur in addition to the primary essence of a person.</note> that a woman has beyond those of the man must be recognized from her own Monarchy, not from the other's. And these same sick- <note>The text cuts off here at the catchword "kranck-" (sick-), to be continued on the following page.</note>

# OF THE MATRIX <note>original: "MATRICIS" (Latin)</note>

They forget that there is a distinction between metals, stones, wood, etc.; so it is here also. Although a Human <note>original: "Mensch"; here referring to the human species as a whole</note> consists of man and woman—two forms and figures—there are also two distinct natures. The woman carries a special *Monarchy* <term>Monarchy: in Paracelsian medicine, this refers to a self-contained, sovereign anatomical or spiritual system that operates by its own unique laws, independent of others</term> both in general and in specific illness. Just as the power of the sea shows itself, which flows up and rises at its appointed time, so too does this Monarchy. Now, what is the cause of the sea’s rising? It is this: that the sea consumes whatever enters into it; it is the death of those things. Thus, all waters are "swallowed" and killed in the sea, and they die within it, just as a human dies in the Earth. Now, whatever consumes and kills has its own *operation* <term>operation: the functional process or "working" of a biological or chemical system</term> through which it breaks down what enters it. Its operation occurs once every day.

In the same way, consider a human who eats: he digests the food he eats, and once he has received it and it has entered into the operation of being consumed, the belly swells up. Thus the sea has its effect, for in the sea is the death of water; no water comes out of the sea again, but everything dies within it, just as all *earth-growths* <note>original: "Erdgewächs"; referring to plants and all things that grow from the soil</note> die in the Earth. Now, just as the sea has its ebbing and its flowing: understand that the same exists within the woman. This is because she is a Mother of children; the sea...

...[neither a] toad nor a snake [would give such poison]: No one: God even less / considering this: It is true, however / that nature stands still within its poison <note>referring to the cessation of menstruation during pregnancy</note> and is restrained / just as the sun was held still in the days of Joshua <note>A biblical reference to Joshua 10:12–13, where the sun stops in the sky to aid a battle; here used to describe the suspension of a woman's natural cycle.</note> / So too does the whole course of nature stand still / from the time of conception / until the child's necessity to change <note>to be born</note> concludes / and [the child] is nourished like a human being: It is also true that at the birth / everything cleanses itself out / so that nothing [unclean] remains / that is / that which you think would become milk / would sooner become a water-calf <note>original: "Wasserkalb"; a historical term for a molar pregnancy or a "false" growth in the womb that results from a corrupted conception.</note>: It is Divine faithfulness / that the child is protected from the harm of such filth and is shielded from it / and yet you say it nourishes itself from that / when no thing has ever been nourished by filth / but rather by pure things / whether they be trees / herbs / roots / etc. Everything is nourished and sustained by subtle liquids <note>original: "subtilen liquoribus"</note> / by dew <note>original: "rore"</note> / by rains <note>original: "pluviis"</note> / etc. Above all else, the child is in its nourishment / for the child is of greater concern than the herb in the field / and is the most delicate thing: Therefore it is entrusted to its mother and her art <note>the natural physiological skill of the female body</note> / until its strength is established / by means of the most subtle foods. Having previously argued that a fetus is not nourished by menstrual blood, he now refutes the idea that this same fluid becomes breast milk after birth, using the analogy of cooking scum to explain bodily waste.</meta>

# THE BOOK

milk, and remains in the womb <term>Matrix; the uterus or "mother-womb"</term> until the child is born, then it becomes milk—one can clearly see from that what kind of philosophy <note>original: *Philosophia*; here used sarcastically to refer to the natural science or medical theories one has been taught</note> you have been washed in! That which rises up—pay close attention—is the foam from the boiling <note>The "cooking" or digestion process of the body.</note> that the child has eaten in the mother’s body, and which it must then suck from the breast. Now you see how the waste <term>Excrement; in this context, the metabolic byproducts or "scum" of development</term> turns into a dross. Do you not have enough sense to see that if one wanted to turn a drink into foam, nothing at all would remain in the end? These are the stern Stoics <note>original: *Stoici austeri*; a reference to rigid, traditionalist scholars who ignore the practical realities of nature.</note>, etc.

Therefore, it is only right to present women’s diseases and health as a separate monarchy <term>Monarchey; Paracelsus’s term for a distinct anatomical and biological "kingdom" or system that operates by its own unique laws</term>, because she is so far removed from the man—not only for the sake of the breasts, the womb <note>original: *Mutter*</note>, or the menses <term>Menstrum</term>, but also because of the entire body, which is created for the sake of the breasts, the womb, and the menses. Therefore, since the body is created for their sake, it is created according to their nature, and not according to a foreign nature—that is, a masculine nature. And if a medicine should happen to help both women and men equally, such as in the plague, cold pains, etc., know that in those cases it only happens through a narcotic <term>Narcotischen</term> way, a stupefying <term>Stupefaurender; numbing or dulling the senses</term> way, or a sweat-inducing <term>Diaphoretischer</term> way. None of these come from the true origin of ordained medicine, because they do not act upon the right—

<meta>catchword: right</meta>

<summary>Paracelsus argues that women's medicine must be treated as a distinct field because the female body is fundamentally organized around the womb and breasts. He dismisses the idea that milk is transformed menstrual blood, viewing it instead as a byproduct of the child's development.</summary>

<keywords>monarchy of women, gynecology, breast milk, womb, Matrix, Stoics, narcotic, diaphoretic, Paracelsian medicine</keywords>

--- Page 197 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the fundamental biological differences between men and women. The previous page ended mid-sentence, arguing that the female body is created according to its own nature rather than a "masculine nature," and if one fails to follow this...</meta>

# OF THE WOMB <note>original: *Matricis*; the genitive form of Matrix, meaning "of the womb" or "of the mother."</note>

...right order, things go wrong. For this reason, a remedy helps today but not tomorrow—in the plague <note>original: *Peſte*</note> as in other illnesses—of which more will be mentioned later in its own chapter, etc.

Since the correct order should now be used, it is only fair to distinguish women by name, along with their health and their sickness, and to consider this example: A woman is like the **Tree** that bears its fruit, and the **Man** is like the **fruit** that the Tree bears. It is to be understood in this way: the Tree must have much until it is sustained, so that it may give that for which it exists. Now see how many ailments <term>gebreſten; archaic term for physical defects, infirmities, or chronic illnesses</term> may belong to the tree and how few to the pears; by that same measure is the woman above the man. In comparison to her, the man is like a pear to its tree, which falls, yet leaves the tree standing. 

Now, the tree takes care again for other fruits and for its own longer life; therefore it must have much, suffer much, and bear much for the sake of its fruit, so that the fruit may come forth well and successfully. Therefore, judge rightly—if you wish to be physicians <term>Arzt; here meaning a learned healer or doctor</term>—what this example contains and how it is to be understood; otherwise, none of you will ever become a good physician. Therefore, behold this Monarchy <term>Monarchey; Paracelsus’s concept of a distinct, self-governing biological system</term> separately, wherein you see what is specifically necessary for the Tree that is not necessary for the pears, and what is necessary for the pears that is not necessary for the Tree.

<meta>catchword: Is [Iſt]</meta>

<summary>Paracelsus uses the metaphor of a tree and its fruit to illustrate the biological complexity of women compared to men, arguing that women's health must be studied as a distinct "monarchy" due to their role in reproduction.</summary>
<keywords>womb, anatomy, physician, monarchy of woman, tree and fruit metaphor, reproductive health, pathology</keywords>

--- Page 198 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the fundamental biological differences between men and women, following the metaphor of the woman as a tree and the man as its fruit. It explores the different origins of their physical bodies based on the creation account in Genesis.</meta>

The Book /

If this is an external sign, so it is also with men and women. By this, know that the differences between boys and girls are to be understood in the same way as the pear and the seed that is within the pear. Just as these two types differ from one another, so also are boys and girls distinct from each other; I leave that to Philosophy <term>Philosophey; here meaning natural philosophy, the study of the nature of the physical world</term>. Now consider within yourselves how humanity’s origin is from above, namely out of the *Limbus* <note>original: *Limbo*; Paracelsus uses this term to refer to the "great mystery" or the primordial mass of matter from which the world was created</note>, but the man and woman are not the same: the woman is the second creature and is not the first, nor was she made at the same time as the first; for this reason, she is ranked after the man.

Since she is now the second creature, and not made directly from the *Limbus*, she is also a different kind of body. For if she were the same body as Adam, she would also have been made from that same *Limbus*. However, because she is meant to govern a different Monarchy <term>Monarchey; Paracelsus’s term for a distinct, self-governing biological and spiritual system</term>, she was subsequently made out of living flesh—flesh that already existed—and yet, out of that flesh, a *different* flesh was made. Just as flesh was created from the *Limbus* (becoming something that had not existed before), so the woman was made from the man’s flesh. Yet she did not remain that same flesh; instead, she is as far removed from the man as the man is from the clay <note>original: *leimen*; loam or clay, referring to the "dust of the ground" from which Adam was formed</note> out of which he was made. This makes it necessary to create a new Theory <note>original: *Theoricam*</note> of womanhood and to treat her separately within her own specific Physics <note>original: *Physica*; the study of the nature and constitution of the body</note>...

<meta>catchword: fica/ (Physics)</meta>

<meta>This page continues Paracelsus's argument that the female body is a distinct biological and spiritual system, fundamentally different from the male body, and critiques traditional medical authorities who treat them as identical.</meta>

<page-num>85</page-num>
-># OF THE WOMB <note>original: *MATRICIS*</note><-
<sig>X</sig>

...to verify <note>completing the word *verificiren* from the previous page</note> and to expose as liars those who claim that [physiology] is the same for women and men; although they [women] certainly appear in great numbers, except for the matter in which the child is placed and those things which concern that same necessity. Now consider that necessity: that she must possess the entire body, and there is not a drop of blood in the body that is not a necessity for the **Matrix** <term>Matrix; in Paracelsian medicine, the womb is not merely an organ, but a creative "world" or "center" that governs the entire female anatomy</term>. Therefore, it is unjust [to say they are the same]; but rather, they have not understood it, and the young ones refuse to understand it.

It feels pleasant and easy for you when you say, "**Galen** <term>Galenus; a 2nd-century Greek physician whose theories dominated Western medicine until Paracelsus’s time</term> says this," or "**Avicenna** <term>Auicenna; a famous Persian physician and philosopher from the Islamic Golden Age</term> says that," and so on. By this you feel blameless, thinking you have hit the mark; you believe that simply because they say it, it must be true. First, prove that the author is truthful, and only then examine him; but this will prove too difficult for you. Since people give credit to lazy authors and hold their words as the **Gospel** <term>Euangelium</term>, and since you are no more virtuous yourself, you rightly stick with such lying and fictional authors; for "like attracts like," and the Devil always drives them together.

Now, just as there is a specific nature in women, and a **Monarchy** <term>Monarchia; Paracelsus’s term for a distinct, self-governing realm of nature or a specific biological system</term>, so there must also be two Monarchies: that is, one Monarchy for women, and another for men. From this follows another knowledge of the Heaven and of both spheres that are within them, the **Microcosms** <term>Microcosmi; the "Little Worlds," referring to the belief that the human body is a miniature reflection of the entire universe</term>.

<meta>catchword: ca</meta>

<summary>Paracelsus argues that women and men belong to two different "Monarchies" or biological systems, asserting that the entire female body is subservient to the "Matrix" (womb). He fiercely critiques contemporary doctors for blindly following the ancient authorities Galen and Avicenna instead of observing nature itself.</summary>
